      def save_pdf_to_static_storage(filename: str, file_bytes: bytes, batch_id: str):
          """Saves the imported PDF file into the static folder for later download and preview."""
          import re
          safe_filename = filename.split('/')[-1].split('\\')[-1]
          # Replace non-alphanumeric (except dots, dashes and underscores) with underscores
          safe_filename = re.sub(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]', '_', safe_filename)
          safe_filename = re.sub(r'_+', '_', safe_filename)
          
          saved_name = f"{batch_id}_{safe_filename}"
          pdf_path = os.path.join(PDFS_DIR, saved_name)
          with open(pdf_path, "wb") as f:
              f.write(file_bytes)
          return saved_name
      
      def get_imported_pdfs_list():
          """Returns list of all imported PDF files saved in static directory."""
          if not os.path.exists(PDFS_DIR):
              return []
          
          pdfs = []
          for fn in os.listdir(PDFS_DIR):
              if not fn.lower().endswith('.pdf'):
                  continue
              full_path = os.path.join(PDFS_DIR, fn)
              stat = os.stat(full_path)
              
              # Extract original filename by removing batch prefix (YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS_)
              orig_name = fn
              if len(fn) > 16 and fn[15] == '_':
                  orig_name = fn[16:]
              elif "original.pdf" in fn:
                  orig_name = fn
                  
              pdfs.append({
                  "filename": fn,
                  "original_name": orig_name,
                  "size": stat.st_size,
                  "created_at": datetime.fromtimestamp(stat.st_mtime).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                  "url": f"/static/uploads/claim_imports/pdfs/{fn}"
              })
          # Sort by created_at newest first
          pdfs.sort(key=lambda x: x["created_at"], reverse=True)
          return pdfs
      
      def delete_imported_pdf(filename: str):
          """Deletes an imported PDF file from disk."""
          pdf_path = os.path.join(PDFS_DIR, filename)
          if os.path.exists(pdf_path):
              os.remove(pdf_path)
              return True
          return False
      
      def validate_import_files(files, db=None):
          """
          Validates that all uploaded PDF files contain at least one valid claim.
          Also checks if claims already exist in DB - blocks import if all claims are duplicates.
          Raises ValueError if any file is invalid, empty, or contains only duplicate data.
          """
          # Get existing keys from DB for duplicate checking
          existing_keys = get_existing_claims_keys(db) if db else set()
          
          total_new_claims = 0
          total_duplicate_claims = 0
          
          for upload_file in files:
              filename = upload_file.filename.split('/')[-1].split('\\')[-1]
              
              # Read file bytes
              file_bytes = upload_file.file.read()
              upload_file.file.seek(0)

              temp_pdf_path = os.path.join(UPLOADS_DIR, temp_pdf_name)
              with open(temp_pdf_path, "wb") as f:
                  f.write(file_bytes)
                  
              try:
                  seen_keys_temp = set()
                  claims, period, parse_warnings, mechanism_info = parse_pdf_claims(temp_pdf_path, "val_batch", "", seen_keys_temp, normalize_penalty_value, claims_only=True)
                  
                  if os.path.exists(temp_pdf_path):
                      os.remove(temp_pdf_path)
                      
                  if not claims or len(claims) == 0:
                      raise ValueError(f"file PDF [{filename}] ko hợp lệ, yêu cầu kiểm tra lại")
                  
                  # Check how many claims are duplicates
                  if existing_keys:
                      for c in claims:
                          try:
                              cab_no = int(c["cabinet_no"]) if c.get("cabinet_no") is not None else None
                          except (ValueError, TypeError):
                              cab_no = c.get("cabinet_no")
                          
                          data_fields = (
                              str(c.get("sku") or "").strip(),
                              str(c.get("claim_description") or "").strip(),
                              str(c.get("defect_code") or "").strip(),
                              normalize_penalty_value(c.get("action")),
                              str(c.get("lpn") or "").strip(),
                              str(c.get("container_no") or "").strip(),
                              str(c.get("period") or "").strip()
                          )
                          key_with_cab = (cab_no,) + data_fields
                          key_without_cab = (None,) + data_fields
                          
                          if key_with_cab in existing_keys or key_without_cab in existing_keys:
                              total_duplicate_claims += 1
                          else:
                              total_new_claims += 1
                  else:
                      total_new_claims += len(claims)
                      
              except Exception as e:
                  if os.path.exists(temp_pdf_path):
                      os.remove(temp_pdf_path)
                  if isinstance(e, ValueError) and "ko hợp lệ" in str(e):
                      raise e
                  raise ValueError(f"Lỗi khi đọc file {filename}: {str(e)}")
          
          # If ALL claims are duplicates, block the import
          if total_new_claims == 0 and total_duplicate_claims > 0:
              raise ValueError(
                  f"Dữ liệu đã tồn tại trong hệ thống! "
                  f"Tất cả {total_duplicate_claims} dòng khiếu nại đã được import trước đó. "
                  f"Không thể import trùng lặp."
              )

      def parse_pdf_claims(pdf_path, batch_id, images_batch_dir, seen_keys=None, normalize_penalty_func=None):
          """
          Parses claims from PDF using PyMuPDF coordinate-based sorting.
          Falls back to OCR if a page contains zero text.
          Extracts and crops defect photos from photo pages.
          
          Args:
              pdf_path: Path to PDF file
              batch_id: Batch identifier
              images_batch_dir: Directory to save cropped images
              seen_keys: Set of existing claim keys for deduplication
              normalize_penalty_func: Function to normalize penalty values (from shared.utils.claim_utils)
    INCOMING (52 lines):
      def parse_pdf_claims(pdf_path, batch_id, images_batch_dir, seen_keys=None, normalize_penalty_func=None, claims_only=False):
          """
          Router entrypoint function: detects PDF format and delegates parsing to Mechanism 1 or Mechanism 2.
          
          Mechanism 1 (standard): PDF has separate data pages (tables) and photo pages 
                                  (identified by 'REFERENCE DEFECTS PHOTOS' header).
          Mechanism 2 (mixed):    PDF has combined data+photos on each page (no separate photo section).
          
          Returns:
              (claims, period, parse_warnings, mechanism_info)
              mechanism_info = {"id": 1 or 2, "name": "...", "description": "..."}
          """
          if seen_keys is None:
              seen_keys = set()
          
          if normalize_penalty_func is None:
              normalize_penalty_func = lambda x: x
          
          filename = os.path.basename(pdf_path)
          doc = fitz.open(pdf_path)
          has_separate_photo_section = False
          detected_on_page = None
          
          for page_num in range(len(doc)):
              page = doc[page_num]
              text = page.get_text()
              # Normalize whitespace (including newlines) for more robust matching
              text_normalized = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', text)
              if re.search(r'REFERENCE\s+DEFECTS?\s+PHOTOS?', text_normalized, re.IGNORECASE) or \
                 re.search(r'H[iì]nh\s+[AẢả]nh\s+C[aá]c\s+L[oỗ]i', text_normalized, re.IGNORECASE) or \
                 re.search(r'REFERENCE\s+PHOTOS?', text_normalized, re.IGNORECASE) or \
                 re.search(r'DEFECTS?\s+PHOTOS?', text_normalized, re.IGNORECASE) or \
                 re.search(r'H[iì]nh\s+[AẢả]nh\s+Minh\s+Ch[uứ]ng', text_normalized, re.IGNORECASE) or \
                 re.search(r'H[iì]nh\s+[AẢả]nh\s+L[oỗ]i', text_normalized, re.IGNORECASE):
                  has_separate_photo_section = True
                  detected_on_page = page_num + 1
                  break
                  
          doc.close()
          
          if has_separate_photo_section:
              logger.info(f"[{filename}] ✓ Phát hiện CƠ CHẾ 1 (standard PDF, trang ảnh riêng biệt phát hiện tại trang {detected_on_page})")
              return parse_pdf_claims_mechanism_1(pdf_path, batch_id, images_batch_dir, seen_keys, normalize_penalty_func, claims_only)
          else:
              logger.info(f"[{filename}] ✓ Phát hiện CƠ CHẾ 2 (mixed PDF, không có trang ảnh riêng biệt)")
              return parse_pdf_claims_mechanism_2(pdf_path, batch_id, images_batch_dir, seen_keys, normalize_penalty_func, claims_only)

      def parse_pdf_claims_mechanism_2(pdf_path, batch_id, images_batch_dir, seen_keys=None, normalize_penalty_func=None, claims_only=False):
          """
          [CƠ CHẾ 2] - Cơ chế mới dành riêng cho file dạng hỗn hợp vừa có bảng claim vừa có ảnh trên từng trang (như file 1111.pdf).
          Xử lý việc cắt ảnh theo các khung viền đen tự động và phân bổ các trang ảnh nối tiếp (overflow) một cách độc lập.
          """
          if seen_keys is None:
              seen_keys = set()
          
          if normalize_penalty_func is None:
              normalize_penalty_func = lambda x: x
          
          doc = fitz.open(pdf_path)
          pdf_name_clean = re.sub(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]', '_', os.path.basename(pdf_path).lower().replace('.pdf', ''))
          claims = []
          parse_warnings = []
          period = None
          
          for page_num in range(len(doc)):
              page = doc[page_num]
              text = page.get_text()
              
              if not text.strip():
                  logger.info(f"Page {page_num + 1} has no text. Trying OCR fallback...")
                  try:
                      text = page.get_text("text", ocr=True)
                  except Exception as ocr_err:
                      msg = f"OCR fallback failed on page {page_num + 1}: {str(ocr_err)}"
                      logger.warning(msg)
                      parse_warnings.append(msg)
              
              # Skip photo-only pages (evidence photo pages contain this header)
              # These pages have "Cabinet#X: SKU / Description" labels but no actual claim data
              if re.search(r'REFERENCE\s+DEFECTS?\s+PHOTOS?', text, re.IGNORECASE) or \
                 re.search(r'Hình\s+Ảnh\s+Các\s+Lỗi', text, re.IGNORECASE):
                  logger.info(f"Page {page_num + 1}: Skipping photo-only page (REFERENCE DEFECTS PHOTOS)")
                  continue
              
              # Also skip pages that have only 1 Cabinet label and no table structure
              # (pure photo pages without the REFERENCE DEFECTS PHOTOS header)
              cab_count_on_page = len(re.findall(r'Cabinet\s*#?\s*\d+', text, re.IGNORECASE))
              has_table_structure = bool(re.search(r'\b(STT|LPN|Container\s*No|Defect\s*Code|Action)\b', text, re.IGNORECASE))
              if cab_count_on_page <= 1 and not has_table_structure and re.search(r'Cabinet\s*#?\s*\d+', text, re.IGNORECASE):
                  logger.info(f"Page {page_num + 1}: Skipping photo-only page (single Cabinet label, no table headers)")
                  continue
                      
              # Extract text blocks
              blocks = page.get_text("blocks")
              text_blocks = [b for b in blocks if b[6] == 0]
              
              # Filter headers
              clean_blocks = []
              for b in text_blocks:
                  text_content = b[4].strip()
                  if "[CLAIM REPORT AT VINA G7 FACTORY]" in text_content or re.search(r'Page\s*\d+\s*of\s*\d+', text_content, re.IGNORECASE):
                      continue
                  clean_blocks.append(b)
                  
              rows = group_blocks_into_rows(clean_blocks)
              
              for row in rows:
                  row_text = " ".join([b[4].strip() for b in row])
                  period_match = re.search(r'THÁNG\s*(\d{1,2})\s*(?:-|_|–|—|/)?\s*(\d{4})', row_text, re.IGNORECASE)
                  if period_match:
                      month_str = period_match.group(1).zfill(2)
                      period = f"{month_str}-{period_match.group(2)}"
                      # CHỈ skip row nếu row KHÔNG chứa Cabinet data
                      # (tránh mất dòng khi banner THÁNG bị gộp chung với Cabinet#1)
                      has_cabinet_in_row = bool(re.search(r'Cabinet\s*#\s*\d+', row_text, re.IGNORECASE))
                      if not has_cabinet_in_row:
                          continue
                      
                  b_texts = [b[4].strip() for b in row]
                  stt = None
                  for text_val in b_texts:
                      if text_val.isdigit() and len(text_val) <= 2:
                          stt = int(text_val)
                          break
                  if stt is None:
                      stt = len(claims) + 1
                      
                  cabinet_no = None
                  sku = ""
                  claim_description_parts = []
                  lpn = ""
                  container_no = ""
                  defect_codes = []
                  actions = []
                  has_cabinet = False
                  
                  for b in row:
                      text_content = b[4].strip()
                      lines = [line.strip() for line in text_content.split('\n') if line.strip()]
                      
                      for line in lines:
                          if re.search(r'Cabinet\s*#\s*\d+', line, re.IGNORECASE):
                              has_cabinet = True
                              cab_match = re.search(r'Cabinet\s*#\s*(\d+)\s*:\s*(.*)', line, re.IGNORECASE)
                              if cab_match:
                                  cabinet_no = int(cab_match.group(1))
                                  sku = cab_match.group(2).strip()
                                  if '/' in sku:
                                      sku = sku.split('/')[0].strip()
                              else:
                                  cab_match_simple = re.search(r'Cabinet\s*#\s*(\d+)', line, re.IGNORECASE)
                                  if cab_match_simple:
                                      cabinet_no = int(cab_match_simple.group(1))
                              continue
                              
                          lpn_match = re.search(r'LPN\s*:?\s*([0-9]+)', line, re.IGNORECASE)
                          if lpn_match:
                              lpn = lpn_match.group(1).strip()
                              continue
                              
                          container_match = re.search(r'Container\s*:?\s*([A-Za-z0-9]+)', line, re.IGNORECASE)
                          if container_match:
                              container_no = container_match.group(1).strip()
                              continue
                              
                          defect_matches = re.findall(r'\b([a-zA-Z]{2,4}[a-zA-Z0-9]*[-_][0-9]+[a-zA-Z0-9_-]*)\b', line, re.IGNORECASE)
                          if defect_matches:
                              for dm in defect_matches:
                                  defect_codes.append(dm.strip())
                              continue
                              
                          action_match = re.search(r'\b(PAY|REPLACE|REPAIR)\b.*', line, re.IGNORECASE)
                          if action_match:
                              actions.append(action_match.group(0).strip())
                              continue
                              
                          if line.isdigit() and len(line) <= 2:
                              continue
                              
                          if line.upper() in ["LPN:", "CONTAINER:", "DEFECTS", "ACTION", "STT", "SKU / CLAIM"]:
                              continue
                              
                          claim_description_parts.append(line)
                          
                  claim_description = " / ".join(claim_description_parts)
                  
                  if not has_cabinet:
                      continue
                      
                  cleaned_actions = []
                  for act in actions:
                      act_clean = re.sub(r'\bPAY\b', '', act, flags=re.IGNORECASE).strip()
                      if re.match(r'^\d+$', act_clean):
                          act_clean = f"${act_clean}"
                      act_clean = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', act_clean).strip()
                      act_clean = normalize_penalty_func(act_clean)
                      if act_clean and act_clean not in cleaned_actions:
                          cleaned_actions.append(act_clean)
                  unique_defect_codes = []
                  for dc in defect_codes:
                      if dc not in unique_defect_codes:
                          unique_defect_codes.append(dc)
                  defect_code = ", ".join(unique_defect_codes)
                  action = ", ".join(cleaned_actions)
      
                  if cabinet_no is not None:
                      claims.append({
                          "id": f"{batch_id}_{len(claims) + 1}",
                          "batch_id": batch_id,
                          "stt": stt,
                          "cabinet_no": cabinet_no,
                          "sku": sku,
                          "claim_description": claim_description,
                          "defect_code": defect_code,
                          "company_error_code": "",
                          "action": action,
                          "lpn": lpn,
                          "container_no": container_no,
                          "period": period,
                          "image_paths": [],
                          "image_captions": [],
                          "source_page": page_num + 1,
                          "photo_pages": [],
                          "status": "Mới"
                      })
                  else:
                      parse_warnings.append(f"Dòng {stt} ở trang {page_num + 1}: Không tìm thấy số Cabinet.")
      
          filename = os.path.basename(pdf_path)
          parsed_count_before_dedup = len(claims)
          logger.info(f"[{filename}] Cơ chế 2: Đã parse được {parsed_count_before_dedup} claims (trước dedup)")
      
          # Deduplicate claims where all key fields are identical
          unique_claims = []
          duplicate_count = 0
          seen_in_file = set()
          
          # Do NOT discard duplicates anymore, keep all and let user inspect them as Exceptions
          for c in claims:
              try:
                  cab_no = int(c["cabinet_no"]) if c["cabinet_no"] is not None else None
              except (ValueError, TypeError):
                  cab_no = c["cabinet_no"]
              
              data_fields = (
                  str(c["sku"] or "").strip(),
                  str(c["claim_description"] or "").strip(),
                  str(c["defect_code"] or "").strip(),
                  normalize_penalty_func(c["action"]),
                  str(c["lpn"] or "").strip(),
                  str(c["container_no"] or "").strip(),
                  str(c["period"] or "").strip()
              )
              key_with_cab = (cab_no,) + data_fields
              
              # Check duplicate 100% locally or in DB
              is_dup_in_file = key_with_cab in seen_in_file
              is_dup_in_db = key_with_cab in seen_keys
              
              if is_dup_in_file or is_dup_in_db:
                  duplicate_count += 1
                  msg = f"Dòng Cabinet #{cab_no} (STT {c['stt']}) bị trùng lặp dữ liệu hoàn toàn 100%."
                  if msg not in parse_warnings:
                      parse_warnings.append(msg)
              
              seen_in_file.add(key_with_cab)
              seen_keys.add(key_with_cab)
              unique_claims.append(c)
          
          logger.info(f"[{filename}] Cơ chế 2: Đã giữ lại toàn bộ {len(unique_claims)} claims (bao gồm {duplicate_count} dòng trùng lặp phát hiện)")
                  
          # Reset stt for the unique claims to be sequential
          for i, c in enumerate(unique_claims):
              c["stt"] = i + 1
              c["id"] = f"{batch_id}_{i + 1}"
              
          claims = unique_claims
      
          # Validate parsing results
          for c in claims:
              cab_no = c["cabinet_no"]
              page_num = c["source_page"]
              if not c["sku"]:
                  parse_warnings.append(f"Cabinet #{cab_no} ở trang {page_num}: Thiếu mã SKU.")
              if not c["period"]:
                  parse_warnings.append(f"Cabinet #{cab_no} ở trang {page_num}: Thiếu kỳ báo cáo (Period).")
              if not c["defect_code"]:
                  parse_warnings.append(f"Cabinet #{cab_no} ở trang {page_num}: Thiếu mã lỗi (DVA_XX).")
              if not c["action"]:
                  parse_warnings.append(f"Cabinet #{cab_no} ở trang {page_num}: Thiếu biện pháp xử lý (Action).")
      
          mechanism_info = {
              "id": 2,
              "name": "Cơ chế 2 (Mixed)",
              "description": "Bảng claim và ảnh minh chứng trộn lẫn trên từng trang"
          }
      
          if claims_only:
              doc.close()
              return claims, period or datetime.now().strftime("%m-%Y"), parse_warnings, mechanism_info
      
          # 2. Xử lý cắt ảnh theo Cơ chế 2 (Mixed pages & overflow pages)
          claim_source_pages = {cl["source_page"] for cl in claims}
          
          for c in claims:
              # 1. Xác định trang nguồn và tất cả các trang nối tiếp của claim này
              start_page_1 = c["source_page"]  # 1-indexed
              pages_to_process = [start_page_1 - 1]  # 0-indexed list of pages to process
              
              # Tìm các trang nối tiếp ngay sau trang nguồn mà không chứa dòng claim nào khác
              next_page_idx = start_page_1
              while next_page_idx < len(doc):
                  # Nếu trang tiếp theo này không phải là trang nguồn của bất kỳ claim nào khác
                  if (next_page_idx + 1) not in claim_source_pages:
                      pages_to_process.append(next_page_idx)
                      next_page_idx += 1
                  else:
                      break
                      
              # 2. Xử lý cắt ảnh trên từng trang trong danh sách
              for page_num in pages_to_process:
                  # Bỏ qua trang bìa đầu tiên (trang 1) nếu nó lọt vào danh sách
                  if page_num == 0:
                      continue
                      
                  page = doc[page_num]
                  
                  # Chỉ xử lý nếu trang có chứa hình ảnh
                  if len(page.get_images()) > 0:
                      try:
                          # Xác định đáy của bảng claim (nếu là trang nguồn) hoặc dùng header_limit (nếu là trang nối tiếp)
                          table_bottom_y = 60
                          blocks = page.get_text("blocks")
                          
                          # Tìm tọa độ dưới cùng của bảng claim ở phần trên trang
                          for b in blocks:
                              x0, y0, x1, y1, text_content, block_no, block_type = b
                              if block_type == 0:
                                  text_upper = text_content.upper()
                                  # Nhận diện các khối chữ thuộc bảng claim ở phía trên trang
                                  if any(k in text_upper for k in ["STT", "SKU", "CLAIM", "DEFECT", "ACTION", "CABINET", "LPN", "CONTAINER", "PAY", "REPAIR", "REPLACE"]):
                                      if y1 > table_bottom_y and y1 < 250:
                                          table_bottom_y = y1
                                          
                          # Lấy danh sách ảnh trên trang
                          image_infos = page.get_image_info(xrefs=True)
                          
                          if image_infos:
                              # Trích xuất các nét vẽ thẳng (đường viền khung đen) nằm bên dưới bảng claim
                              drawings = page.get_drawings()
                              v_lines = []
                              h_lines = []
                              
                              for d in drawings:
                                  r = d['rect']
                                  if r.y0 < table_bottom_y + 5:
                                      continue
                                  # Nét dọc
                                  if r.width <= 3 and r.height >= 30:
                                      v_lines.append(r)
                                  # Nét ngang
                                  elif r.height <= 3 and r.width >= 30:
                                      h_lines.append(r)
                                      
                              # Với mỗi ảnh, tìm đường biên khung đen bao quanh gần nhất để cắt
                              for img_idx, img in enumerate(image_infos):
                                  bbox = img['bbox']
                                  img_x0, img_y0, img_x1, img_y1 = bbox
                                  
                                  # Bỏ qua nếu ảnh nằm trong vùng bảng dữ liệu phía trên
                                  if img_y0 < table_bottom_y:
                                      continue
                                      
                                  left_candidates = [vl.x0 for vl in v_lines if vl.x0 <= img_x0 + 2 and abs(vl.x0 - img_x0) < 25]
                                  crop_x0 = min(left_candidates, key=lambda x: abs(x - img_x0)) if left_candidates else img_x0 - 7
                                  
                                  right_candidates = [vl.x1 for vl in v_lines if vl.x1 >= img_x1 - 2 and abs(vl.x1 - img_x1) < 25]
                                  crop_x1 = min(right_candidates, key=lambda x: abs(x - img_x1)) if right_candidates else img_x1 + 7
                                  
                                  top_candidates = [hl.y0 for hl in h_lines if hl.y0 <= img_y0 + 2 and abs(hl.y0 - img_y0) < 25]
                                  crop_y0 = min(top_candidates, key=lambda y: abs(y - img_y0)) if top_candidates else img_y0 - 2
                                  
                                  bottom_candidates = [hl.y1 for hl in h_lines if hl.y1 >= img_y1 - 2 and abs(hl.y1 - img_y1) < 25]
                                  crop_y1 = min(bottom_candidates, key=lambda y: abs(y - img_y1)) if bottom_candidates else img_y1 + 2
                                  
                                  crop_rect = fitz.Rect(crop_x0, crop_y0, crop_x1, crop_y1)
                                  crop_rect = crop_rect & page.rect
                                  
                                  if not crop_rect.is_empty and crop_rect.width > 2 and crop_rect.height > 2:
                                      pix = page.get_pixmap(clip=crop_rect, dpi=150)
                                      cab_no = c["cabinet_no"]
                                      
                                      # Đặt tên phân biệt ảnh chính và ảnh nối tiếp
                                      is_continuation = page_num != (start_page_1 - 1)
                                      suffix = f"photo_cont_{img_idx + 1}" if is_continuation else f"photo_{img_idx + 1}"
                                      caption_desc = f"Ảnh nối tiếp {img_idx + 1}" if is_continuation else f"Ảnh {img_idx + 1}"
                                      
                                      img_filename = f"{pdf_name_clean}_cabinet_{cab_no}_page_{page_num + 1}_{suffix}.png"
                                      img_path = os.path.join(images_batch_dir, img_filename)
                                      pix.save(img_path)
                                      
                                      c["image_paths"].append(f"/static/uploads/claim_imports/images/{batch_id}/{img_filename}")
                                      c["image_captions"].append(f"Cabinet #{cab_no} ({caption_desc} trang {page_num + 1})")
                                      c["photo_pages"].append(page_num + 1)
                                      
                          # Nếu vì lý do nào đó không trích xuất được ảnh riêng lẻ, cắt toàn bộ vùng ảnh làm dự phòng
                          has_page_img = any(f"page_{page_num + 1}" in p for p in c.get("image_paths", []))
                          if not has_page_img:
                              y_start = table_bottom_y + 10
                              y_end = page.rect.height - 30
                              x_start = max(0, 30)
                              x_end = max(x_start + 1, page.rect.width - 30)
                              
                              y_start_clamped = max(0, y_start)
                              y_end_clamped = min(page.rect.height, y_end)
                              
                              if y_end_clamped > y_start_clamped + 20 and x_end > x_start:
                                  crop_rect = fitz.Rect(x_start, y_start_clamped, x_end, y_end_clamped)
                                  crop_rect = crop_rect & page.rect
                                  
                                  if not crop_rect.is_empty and crop_rect.width > 1 and crop_rect.height > 1:
                                      pix = page.get_pixmap(clip=crop_rect, dpi=150)
                                      cab_no = c["cabinet_no"]
                                      
                                      is_continuation = page_num != (start_page_1 - 1)
                                      name_suffix = "continuation" if is_continuation else "mixed"
                                      caption_desc = "Ảnh bổ sung" if is_continuation else "Ảnh minh chứng"
                                      
                                      img_filename = f"{pdf_name_clean}_cabinet_{cab_no}_page_{page_num + 1}_{name_suffix}.png"
                                      img_path = os.path.join(images_batch_dir, img_filename)
                                      pix.save(img_path)
                                      
                                      c["image_paths"].append(f"/static/uploads/claim_imports/images/{batch_id}/{img_filename}")
                                      c["image_captions"].append(f"Cabinet #{cab_no} ({caption_desc} trang {page_num + 1})")
                                      c["photo_pages"].append(page_num + 1)
                                      
                      except Exception as img_err:
                          logger.warning(f"Bỏ qua cắt ảnh hỗn hợp trang {page_num + 1} cho Cabinet #{c['cabinet_no']}: {str(img_err)}")
      
          doc.close()
          logger.info(f"[{os.path.basename(pdf_path)}] Cơ chế 2: Kết quả cuối cùng = {len(claims)} claims")
          return claims, period or datetime.now().strftime("%m-%Y"), parse_warnings, mechanism_info

          const RADIAN = Math.PI / 180;
      
          const renderPremiumLabel = (props) => {
            const { cx, cy, midAngle, outerRadius, name, percent } = props;
            if (!name) return null;
      
            const radius = outerRadius + 18;
            const x = cx + radius * Math.cos(-midAngle * RADIAN);
            const y = cy + radius * Math.sin(-midAngle * RADIAN);
      
            const lineEnd = outerRadius + 8;
            const ex = cx + lineEnd * Math.cos(-midAngle * RADIAN);
            const ey = cy + lineEnd * Math.sin(-midAngle * RADIAN);

            const displayName = name.length > 16 ? name.substring(0, 14) + '…' : name;

              <g>
                <line x1={ex} y1={ey} x2={x} y2={y} stroke="#BCAAA4" strokeWidth={0.8} />
                <circle cx={x} cy={y} r={1.5} fill="#8D6E63" />
                <text x={x + (x > cx ? 4 : -4)} y={y - 2} textAnchor={anchor} fill="#5D4037" fontSize={9} fontWeight={600}>
                  {displayName}
                </text>
                <text x={x + (x > cx ? 4 : -4)} y={y + 9} textAnchor={anchor} fill="#A0301B" fontSize={8} fontWeight={700}>
                  {pctText}
                </text>
              </g>
            );
          };

      const renderMessageText = (text) => {
        if (!text) return null;
      
        const chartRegex = /<chart\s+type="([^"]+)"\s+title="([^"]+)"\s*>([\s\S]*?)<\/chart>/g;
        const parts = [];
        let lastIndex = 0;
        let match;
      
        while ((match = chartRegex.exec(text)) !== null) {
          if (match.index > lastIndex) {
            parts.push({
              type: 'text',
              content: text.substring(lastIndex, match.index)
            });
          }
      
          const chartType = match[1];
          const chartTitle = match[2];
          const chartDataRaw = match[3];
          let chartData = [];
          try {
            chartData = JSON.parse(chartDataRaw.trim());
          } catch (e) {
            console.error("Failed to parse chart JSON:", e);
          }
      
          parts.push({
            type: 'chart',
            chartType,
            chartTitle,
            data: chartData
          });
      
          lastIndex = chartRegex.lastIndex;
        }
      
        if (lastIndex < text.length) {
          parts.push({
            type: 'text',
            content: text.substring(lastIndex)
          });
        }
      
        if (parts.length === 0) {
          parts.push({ type: 'text', content: text });
        }
      
        return (
          <div>
            {parts.map((part, index) => {
              if (part.type === 'chart') {
                return renderChartComponent(part.chartType, part.chartTitle, part.data, index);
              } else {
                const hasHtml = part.content.includes('<table') || part.content.includes('<img') || part.content.includes('<div') || part.content.includes('<p');
                if (hasHtml) {
                  return <div key={index} dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: part.content }} />;
                } else {
                  const mdImageRegex = /!\[([^\]]*)\]\(([^)]+)\)/g;
                  if (mdImageRegex.test(part.content)) {
                    const subparts = [];
                    let subLastIndex = 0;
                    let subMatch;
                    mdImageRegex.lastIndex = 0;
                    
                    while ((subMatch = mdImageRegex.exec(part.content)) !== null) {
                      if (subMatch.index > subLastIndex) {
                        subparts.push(part.content.substring(subLastIndex, subMatch.index));
                      }
                      const alt = subMatch[1];
                      const src = subMatch[2];
                      subparts.push(
                        <div key={`img-${subMatch.index}`} className="chat-rendered-image-container" style={{ margin: '12px 0' }}>
                          <img 
                            src={src} 
                            alt={alt} 
                            style={{ 
                              maxWidth: '100%', 
                              borderRadius: '10px', 
                              border: '1px solid rgba(160, 48, 27, 0.15)',
                              boxShadow: '0 6px 16px rgba(45, 26, 18, 0.12)',
                              display: 'block'
                            }} 
                          />
                          {alt && <div style={{ fontSize: '0.76rem', color: '#8D6E63', textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '6px', fontStyle: 'italic' }}>{alt}</div>}
                        </div>
                      );
                      subLastIndex = mdImageRegex.lastIndex;
                    }
                    if (subLastIndex < part.content.length) {
                      subparts.push(part.content.substring(subLastIndex));
                    }
                    return (
                      <div key={index}>
                        {subparts.map((sp, spIdx) => {
                          if (typeof sp === 'string') {
                            return sp.split('\n').map((line, lIdx) => (
                              <div key={lIdx} style={{ marginBottom: line.trim() === '' ? '6px' : '2px' }}>{line}</div>
                            ));
                          }
                          return sp;
                        })}
                      </div>
                    );
                  }
      
                  return (
                    <div key={index}>
                      {part.content.split('\n').map((line, lIdx, arr) => (
                        <div key={lIdx} style={{ marginBottom: lIdx === arr.length - 1 ? '0px' : (line.trim() === '' ? '6px' : '2px') }}>{line}</div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  );
                }
              }
            })}
          </div>
        );
      };

        // Floating button dragging states (default: right 24px, bottom 100px - which is higher up)
        const [btnPosition, setBtnPosition] = useState({ right: 24, bottom: 100 });
        const isDraggingRef = useRef(false);

        const handleMouseDown = (e) => {
          if (e.button !== 0) return; // Only left click
          isDraggingRef.current = false;
          dragStart.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
          positionStart.current = { ...btnPosition };
      
          const handleMouseMove = (moveEvent) => {
            const dx = moveEvent.clientX - dragStart.current.x;
            const dy = moveEvent.clientY - dragStart.current.y;
      
            if (Math.abs(dx) > 5 || Math.abs(dy) > 5) {
              isDraggingRef.current = true;
            }
      
            const newRight = Math.max(10, Math.min(window.innerWidth - 66, positionStart.current.right - dx));
            const newBottom = Math.max(10, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 66, positionStart.current.bottom - dy));
      
            setBtnPosition({ right: newRight, bottom: newBottom });
          };
      
          const handleMouseUp = () => {
            document.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
            document.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
          };
      
          document.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
          document.addEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
        };
      
        const handleTouchStart = (e) => {
          isDraggingRef.current = false;
          const touch = e.touches[0];
          dragStart.current = { x: touch.clientX, y: touch.clientY };

          const handleMouseUp = () => {

            document.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);

          document.addEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);

          resizeStart.current = { x: touch.clientX, y: touch.clientY };
          sizeStart.current = { ...panelSize };
      
          const handleTouchMove = (moveEvent) => {
            const touchMove = moveEvent.touches[0];
            const dx = touchMove.clientX - resizeStart.current.x;
            const dy = touchMove.clientY - resizeStart.current.y;
      
            const newWidth = Math.max(320, Math.min(window.innerWidth - 48, sizeStart.current.width - dx));
            const newHeight = Math.max(400, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 48, sizeStart.current.height - dy));
      
            setPanelSize({ width: newWidth, height: newHeight });
          };
      
          const handleTouchEnd = () => {
            setIsResizing(false);
            document.removeEventListener('touchmove', handleTouchMove);
            document.removeEventListener('touchend', handleTouchEnd);
          };
      
          document.addEventListener('touchmove', handleTouchMove);
          document.addEventListener('touchend', handleTouchEnd);
        };
      
        const handlePanelHeaderMouseDown = (e) => {
          if (e.button !== 0) return; // Only left click
          if (e.target.closest('button')) return; // Ignore buttons
      
          dragStart.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
          positionStart.current = { ...btnPosition };
      
          const handleMouseMove = (moveEvent) => {
            const dx = moveEvent.clientX - dragStart.current.x;
            const dy = moveEvent.clientY - dragStart.current.y;
      
            const newRight = Math.max(10, Math.min(window.innerWidth - 100, positionStart.current.right - dx));
            const newBottom = Math.max(10, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 100, positionStart.current.bottom - dy));
      
            setBtnPosition({ right: newRight, bottom: newBottom });
          };
      
          const handleMouseUp = () => {
            document.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
            document.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
          };
      
          document.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
          document.addEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
        };
      
        const handlePanelHeaderTouchStart = (e) => {
          if (e.target.closest('button')) return;
      
          const touch = e.touches[0];
          dragStart.current = { x: touch.clientX, y: touch.clientY };
          positionStart.current = { ...btnPosition };
      
          const handleTouchMove = (moveEvent) => {
            const touchMove = moveEvent.touches[0];
            const dx = touchMove.clientX - dragStart.current.x;
            const dy = touchMove.clientY - dragStart.current.y;
      
            const newRight = Math.max(10, Math.min(window.innerWidth - 100, positionStart.current.right - dx));
            const newBottom = Math.max(10, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 100, positionStart.current.bottom - dy));
      
            setBtnPosition({ right: newRight, bottom: newBottom });
          };
      
          const handleTouchEnd = () => {
            document.removeEventListener('touchmove', handleTouchMove);
            document.removeEventListener('touchend', handleTouchEnd);
          };
      
          document.addEventListener('touchmove', handleTouchMove);
          document.addEventListener('touchend', handleTouchEnd);
        };
      
        // Handle query parameter ?chat=true to auto-open chatbot
        useEffect(() => {
          const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
          if (params.get('chat') === 'true') {
            setIsChatOpen(true);
            const cleanSearch = location.search.replace(/[?&]chat=true/, '').replace(/^\?$/, '');
            navigate({ pathname: location.pathname, search: cleanSearch }, { replace: true });
          }
        }, [location.search]);
      
        // Dynamic welcome suggestions depending on location.pathname
        const getDefaultSuggestions = (pathname) => {
          if (pathname.includes('/admin/prices')) {
            return [
              'Bảng giá tủ đền bù ở đâu?',
              'Tủ nào đền bù đắt nhất?',
              'Báo giá chất liệu WOA',
              'So sánh giá tủ MFC và MDF'
            ];
          } else if (pathname.includes('/manufacture')) {
            return [
              'Kế hoạch sản xuất tháng này',
              'Đơn hàng nào đang sản xuất?',
              'Tỷ lệ QC đạt bao nhiêu?',
              'Danh sách lỗi gỗ tại xưởng'
            ];
          } else {
            return [
              'Tháng 2 có bao nhiêu claim?',
              'Cabinet 12 bị lỗi gì?',
              'Claim nào bị Full BC?',
              'SKU nào bị claim nhiều nhất?',
              'Có claim nào thiếu ảnh lỗi không?'
            ];
          }
        };
      
        const getWelcomeMessage = (pathname) => {
          const sugs = getDefaultSuggestions(pathname);
          return {
            role: 'bot',
            text: 'Xin chào! Tôi có thể giúp gì cho bạn hôm nay? Tôi có thể tra cứu khiếu nại (claims), sản xuất, bàn giao, QC và KPI.',
            suggestions: sugs,
            time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
          };
        };
      
        // Auto-scroll chatbot window to bottom
        useEffect(() => {
          if (isChatOpen && chatMessagesEndRef.current) {
            const timer = setTimeout(() => {
              chatMessagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
            }, 100);
            return () => clearTimeout(timer);
          }
        }, [chatMessages, isChatLoading, isChatOpen]);
      
        // Click outside chatbot drawer to close it
        useEffect(() => {
          const handleClickOutside = (e) => {
            if (
              chatbotDrawerRef.current && 
              !chatbotDrawerRef.current.contains(e.target) &&
              !e.target.closest('.chatbot-ai-emoji-btn')
            ) {
              setIsChatOpen(false);
            }
          };
          document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
          return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
        }, []);
      
        // Fetch all chat sessions
        const loadSessions = async () => {
          try {
            const res = await claimService.getChatSessions();
            if (res && res.sessions) {
              setSessions(res.sessions);
            }
          } catch (err) {
            console.error('Không thể tải danh sách phiên chat:', err);
          }
        };
      
        // Fetch chat history for selected session
        const loadHistory = async (sessionId) => {
          setIsChatLoading(true);
          try {
            const history = await claimService.getClaimChatHistory(sessionId);
            if (history && history.length > 0) {
              const formattedMessages = history.map(h => ({
                role: h.role === 'user' ? 'user' : 'bot',
                text: h.content,
                items: h.items || [],
                suggestions: h.suggestions || [],
                time: h.created_at 
                  ? new Date(h.created_at).toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) 
                  : new Date().toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
              }));
              setChatMessages(formattedMessages);
            } else {
              setChatMessages([getWelcomeMessage(location.pathname)]);
            }
          } catch (err) {
            console.error('Không thể tải lịch sử chat:', err);
            setChatMessages([getWelcomeMessage(location.pathname)]);
          } finally {
            setIsChatLoading(false);
          }
        };
      
        // Load history and sessions list when chat opens or session switches
        useEffect(() => {
          if (isChatOpen) {
            loadSessions();
            loadHistory(currentSessionId);
          }
        }, [isChatOpen, currentSessionId]);
      
        // Handle switching session
        const handleSwitchSession = (sessionId) => {
          setCurrentSessionId(sessionId);
          setShowSessionsPanel(false);
        };
      
        // Handle creating new session
        const handleCreateSession = () => {
          const newSessionId = 'session_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
          setCurrentSessionId(newSessionId);
          setChatMessages([getWelcomeMessage(location.pathname)]);
          setChatContext({});
          setShowSessionsPanel(false);
        };
      
        // Handle deleting session
        const handleDeleteSession = async (sessionId, e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          try {
            await claimService.deleteChatSession(sessionId);
            if (currentSessionId === sessionId) {
              setCurrentSessionId('default');
            }
            loadSessions();
          } catch (err) {
            console.error('Lỗi khi xóa phiên chat:', err);
          }
        };
      
        // Handle clear chat history
        const handleClearChat = async () => {
          try {
            await claimService.clearClaimChatHistory(currentSessionId);
          } catch (err) {
            console.error('Lỗi khi xóa lịch sử chat:', err);
          }
          setChatMessages([getWelcomeMessage(location.pathname)]);
          setChatContext({});
        };
      
        // Handle sending chat message
        const handleSendChatMessage = async (textToSend) => {
          const text = textToSend || (chatInputRef.current ? chatInputRef.current.value : '');
          if (!text || !text.trim()) return;
      
          if (chatInputRef.current) chatInputRef.current.value = '';
      
          const userMsg = {
            role: 'user',
            text: text,
            time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
          };
          setChatMessages(prev => [...prev, userMsg]);
          setIsChatLoading(true);
      
          try {
            const historyToSend = chatMessages
              .filter(m => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'bot')
              .slice(-6)
              .map(m => ({
                role: m.role === 'user' ? 'user' : 'assistant',
                content: (m.text || '').slice(0, 300)
              }));
      
            const contextWithPage = {
              ...chatContext,
              current_page: location.pathname
            };
      
            const response = await claimService.sendClaimChatMessage(text, contextWithPage, historyToSend, currentSessionId);
      
            if (response.context) {
              setChatContext(response.context);
            }
      
            const botMsg = {
              role: 'bot',
              text: response.answer || 'Không nhận được câu trả lời từ máy chủ.',
              items: response.items || [],
              suggestions: response.suggestions || [],
              time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
            };
            setChatMessages(prev => [...prev, botMsg]);
            loadSessions();
          } catch (err) {
            console.error('Lỗi khi chat với Assistant:', err);
            const isTimeout = err.code === 'ECONNABORTED' || (err.message && err.message.toLowerCase().includes('timeout'));
            const isNetwork = err.message && err.message.toLowerCase().includes('network');
            let errorText = 'Không thể kết nối máy chủ.';
            if (isTimeout) {
              errorText = 'Yêu cầu đã hết thời gian chờ. Vui lòng thử lại.';
            } else if (isNetwork) {
              errorText = 'Lỗi kết nối mạng. Vui lòng kiểm tra kết nối và thử lại.';
            } else if (err.response?.data?.detail) {
              errorText = err.response.data.detail;
            }
            const errorMsg = {
              role: 'bot',
              text: errorText,
              suggestions: ['Thử lại'],
              time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
            };
            setChatMessages(prev => [...prev, errorMsg]);
          } finally {
            setIsChatLoading(false);
          }
        };
      
        const handleViewPhoto = (item) => {
          if (location.pathname === '/admin/claims') {
            const event = new CustomEvent('claim-chat-view-photo', { detail: item });
            window.dispatchEvent(event);
          } else {
            sessionStorage.setItem('pending-chat-detail-item', JSON.stringify(item));
            navigate('/admin/claims');
          }
        };
      
        const handleDeleteMessage = (idx) => {
          setChatMessages(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx));
        };
      
      
        // --- OUTSIDE CLICK FOR NOTIFICATIONS ---
        useEffect(() => {
          const handleClickOutside = (e) => {

          return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);

        // --- LOGOUT HANDLER ---
        const handleLogout = () => {
          authService.logout();
          navigate('/login');
        };
      
        // --- MODAL DIALOG DISPLAY ---
        const showModal = (title, message, type = 'info') => {
          setModalConfig({ visible: true, title, message, type });
        };
      
        // --- GLOBAL MULTI-UPLOAD STATE HANDLERS ---
        const updateUpload = (id, delta) => {
          setUploads(prev => prev.map(u => u.id === id ? { ...u, ...delta } : u));
        };
      
        const removeUpload = (id) => {
          setTimeout(() => {
            setUploads(prev => prev.filter(u => u.id !== id));
          }, 5000);
        };
      
        const startGlobalUpload = async (file, endpoint, onComplete) => {
          const uploadId = Date.now().toString();
          const newUpload = {
            id: uploadId,
            active: true,
            progress: 0,
            processed: 0,
            total: 0,
            fileName: file.name,
            status: 'uploading'
          };
      
          setUploads(prev => [...prev, newUpload]);
          await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 100));
      
          const formData = new FormData();
          formData.append('file', file);
      
          try {
            const { API_URL } = await import('@webShared/Services/api.service');
            const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
            const authHeaders = { 'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true' };
            if (token && token !== 'null' && token !== 'undefined') {
              authHeaders['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
            }
      
            const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}${endpoint.replace(/^\/+/, '')}`, {
              method: 'POST',
              body: formData,
              headers: authHeaders
            });
      
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Lỗi kết nối server');
      
            const reader = response.body.getReader();
            const decoder = new TextDecoder();
            let partialLine = '';
      
            while (true) {
              const { value, done } = await reader.read();
              if (done) break;
      
              const chunk = decoder.decode(value || new Uint8Array(), { stream: true });
              const lines = (partialLine + chunk).split('\n');
              partialLine = lines.pop() || '';
      
              for (const line of lines) {
                if (!line.trim()) continue;
                try {
                  const data = JSON.parse(line);
                  if (data.total !== undefined) updateUpload(uploadId, { total: data.total });
                  if (data.processed !== undefined) {
                    updateUpload(uploadId, { processed: data.processed });
                    setUploads(prev => prev.map(u => {
                      if (u.id === uploadId) {
                        const p = Math.round((data.processed / (u.total || 1)) * 100);
                        return { ...u, progress: p > 100 ? 100 : p };
                      }
                      return u;
                    }));
                  }
                  if (data.status === 'error') {
                    updateUpload(uploadId, { status: 'error' });
                    showModal("Lỗi", data.message || `Lỗi khi xử lý file ${file.name}`, "error");
                    removeUpload(uploadId);
                    return;
                  }
                  if (data.status === 'completed') {
                    updateUpload(uploadId, { status: 'completed', progress: 100 });
                    showModal("Thành công", `Đã xử lý xong file ${file.name}`, "success");
                    if (onComplete) onComplete(data);
                    removeUpload(uploadId);
                    return;
                  }
                } catch (e) {}
              }
            }
            throw new Error('Kết nối bị đóng đột ngột trước khi nhận được trạng thái hoàn thành.');
          } catch (error) {
            console.error("Lỗi upload ngầm:", error);
            updateUpload(uploadId, { status: 'error' });
            showModal("Lỗi", `Không thể upload file ${file.name}`, "error");
            removeUpload(uploadId);
          }
        };
      
        // --- MEMOIZE CONTEXT TO PREVENT UNNECESSARY CHILD RE-RENDERS ---
        const outletContext = React.useMemo(() => ({
          setCustomTitle, 
          setCustomSubtitle, 
          setCustomScreenCode,
          setHeaderExtra, 
          showModal,
          startGlobalUpload,
          activeUploads: uploads.length
        }), [uploads.length]); // Only update when upload count changes, setters are stable
      
        // --- PREVIEW MODE HANDLER ---
        const queryParams = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
        const isPreview = queryParams.get('preview') === 'true';
      
        if (isPreview) {
          return (

                setCustomScreenCode: () => {},
                setHeaderExtra: () => {}, 
                showModal,
                startGlobalUpload: () => {},
                activeUploads: 0
              }} />
            </div>
          );
        }
      
        // --- DYNAMIC HEADER TITLE MANAGEMENT ---
        const getPageTitles = () => {
          if (customTitle) {
            return { 
              screenCode: customScreenCode || '', 
              title: customTitle, 
              subtitle: customSubtitle || (isAdmin ? 'ERP G7 Admin' : 'ERP G7 Manufacture') 
            };
          }
          return getScreenInfo(location.pathname);
        };
      
        const { screenCode, title, subtitle } = getPageTitles();
      
        // Reset custom headers on transition
        useEffect(() => {
          setCustomTitle(null);
          setCustomSubtitle(null);
          setCustomScreenCode(null);
          setHeaderExtra(null);
        }, [location.pathname]);
      
        const pathSegments = location.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
        const showBackButton = pathSegments.length >= 3;
      
        return (
          <div 
            className={`admin-layout ${layoutMode === 'fit-to-screen' && scaleFactor !== 1 ? 'fit-to-screen-active' : ''}`}
            style={layoutMode === 'fit-to-screen' && scaleFactor !== 1 ? {
              transform: `scale(${scaleFactor})`,
              transformOrigin: 'top left',
              width: `${100 / scaleFactor}%`,
              maxWidth: 'none',
              height: `${100 / scaleFactor}vh`,
              minHeight: `${100 / scaleFactor}vh`,
              '--scale-factor': scaleFactor
            } : {}}
          >
            {/* Backdrop for Mobile Sidebar */}
            {sidebarActive && (
              <div 
                className="sidebar-backdrop" 
                onClick={() => setSidebarActive(false)}
              ></div>
            )}
      
            {/* Unified State-Aware Sidebar */}
            <Sidebar 
              active={sidebarActive} 
              collapsed={sidebarCollapsed}
              onToggleCollapse={toggleSidebarCollapse}
              onLogout={handleLogout} 
              onClose={() => setSidebarActive(false)}
              uploads={uploads}
            />
      
            {/* Main Content Pane */}
            <div className={`admin-main ${sidebarCollapsed ? 'sidebar-collapsed' : ''}`}>
              {/* Header Section */}
              <header className="admin-header">
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', width: '100%' }}>
                  <div className="header-left">
                    <button 
                      className="toggle-sidebar-btn"
                      onClick={() => {
                        if (window.innerWidth >= 993) {
                          toggleSidebarCollapse();
                        } else {
                          setSidebarActive(!sidebarActive);
                        }
                      }}
                    >
                      <Menu size={20} />
                    </button>
                    <div className="header-titles">
                      <h2 className="header-page-title">{title}</h2>
                      <p className="header-page-subtitle">
                        {screenCode && <span>{screenCode} · </span>}
                        {subtitle}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
      
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '15px' }}>
                    {headerExtra && <div className="header-extra-content">{headerExtra}</div>}
      
                    {/* Back button logic */}
                    {showBackButton && (
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => navigate(-1)}

                          background: 'none',

                        color: '#7B5233',

                        fontWeight: '600',

                        boxShadow: '0 2px 4px rgba(123, 82, 51, 0.05)'
                      }}
                      onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                        e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#7B5233';
                        e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#FAF6F0';
                      }}
                      onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                        e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#EADCC9';
                      }}
                    >
                      <Monitor size={16} />

                      </span>

                          onClick={() => setShowNotifications(!showNotifications)}

        importFolder: async (formData) => {
          return await api.post('/claims/claims/import-folder', formData, {
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'multipart/form-data'
            }
          });
        },
      
        /**
         * SSE streaming folder import - sends FormData and reads SSE progress events.
         * Uses native fetch (not axios) to support streaming response parsing.
         * @param {FormData} formData - with 'files' entries
         * @param {function} onProgress - callback(eventData) for each SSE event
         * @returns {Promise<object>} - the final result payload
         */
        importFolderStream: async (formData, onProgress) => {
          const url = `${API_URL}claims/claims/import-folder-stream`;
          
          const response = await fetch(url, {
            method: 'POST',
            body: formData,
            headers: {
              'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
            },
          });
      
          if (!response.ok) {
            const errorText = await response.text();
            let detail = 'Lỗi kết nối máy chủ.';
            try {
              const errorJson = JSON.parse(errorText);
              detail = errorJson.detail || detail;
            } catch {}
            throw new Error(detail);
          }
      
          const reader = response.body.getReader();
          const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8');
          let finalResult = null;
          let buffer = '';
      
          while (true) {
            const { done, value } = await reader.read();
            if (done) break;
      
            buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
            
            // Parse SSE events from buffer
            const lines = buffer.split('\n');
            buffer = lines.pop() || ''; // Keep incomplete line in buffer
            
            for (const line of lines) {
              if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
                try {
                  const eventData = JSON.parse(line.slice(6));
                  if (eventData.type === 'result') {
                    finalResult = eventData;
                  }
                  if (onProgress) {
                    onProgress(eventData);
                  }
                } catch (e) {
                  // Skip malformed events
                }
              }
            }
          }
      
          // Process remaining buffer
          if (buffer.startsWith('data: ')) {
            try {
              const eventData = JSON.parse(buffer.slice(6));
              if (eventData.type === 'result') {
                finalResult = eventData;
              }
              if (onProgress) {
                onProgress(eventData);
              }
            } catch {}
          }
      
          if (!finalResult) {
            throw new Error('Không nhận được kết quả từ máy chủ.');
          }
      
          return finalResult;
        },
        
      <<<<<<< HEAD

        const getMonthYear = (claim) => {
          const dateStr = claim.date || claim.period || '';
          if (!dateStr) return 'N/A';
          if (/^\d{2}-\d{4}$/.test(dateStr)) {
            return dateStr;
          }
          const match = dateStr.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})/);
          if (match) {
            return `${match[2]}-${match[1]}`;
          }
          return dateStr;
        };
      
        const analyticsData = useMemo(() => {
          const monthlyMap = {};
          filteredClaimsToDisplay.forEach(c => {
            const month = getMonthYear(c);
            if (!monthlyMap[month]) {
              monthlyMap[month] = { month, count: 0, totalAmount: 0 };
            }

            monthlyMap[month].totalAmount += calculateTotalPenalty(c);
          });

            if (b === 'N/A') return -1;
            const [aMonth, aYear] = a.split('-').map(Number);
            const [bMonth, bYear] = b.split('-').map(Number);

            return aMonth - bMonth;
          });

              const tokens = customerDefectCode.split(/[\\,\/;]|\s+/).map(t => t.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean);
              const unmatchedCodes = tokens.filter(token => {
                return !defectCatalog.some(d => {
                  const catalogCode = (d.defect_code || '').trim().toUpperCase();
                  return catalogCode === token || catalogCode.replace('-', '_') === token.replace('-', '_');
                });
              });
              if (unmatchedCodes.length > 0) {
                claimExceptions.push({
                  type: 'missing_defect_code',
                  label: 'Mã lỗi KH không có trong danh mục',
                  detail: `Mã lỗi: ${unmatchedCodes.join(', ')} — không tìm thấy trong danh sách mã lỗi hệ thống.`,
                  severity: 'warning'
                });
              }
            } else if (!customerDefectCode) {
              claimExceptions.push({
                type: 'no_defect_code',
                label: 'Không có mã lỗi KH',
                detail: 'Claim này chưa được gán mã lỗi khách hàng.',
                severity: 'info'
              });
            }
      
            // 2. Có giá trị phạt nhưng tổng phạt = 0
            const penaltyRaw = normalizePenaltyValue(claim.penalty || 'Không có');
            const penaltyLower = penaltyRaw.toLowerCase();
            const hasPenaltyKeyword = penaltyLower.includes('x2 unit') || penaltyLower.includes('x2unit') ||
              penaltyLower.includes('2x unit') || penaltyLower.includes('x2') ||
              penaltyLower.includes('full bc') || penaltyLower.includes('ful bc');
            const totalPenalty = calculateTotalPenalty(claim);
      
            if (hasPenaltyKeyword && totalPenalty === 0) {
              const sku = claim.product_name || claim.sku || 'N/A';
              claimExceptions.push({
                type: 'penalty_no_total',
                label: 'Có giá trị phạt nhưng không tính được tổng phạt',
                detail: `Giá trị phạt: "${penaltyRaw}" — SKU "${sku}" không có trong bảng giá tủ (Module Kinh doanh). Cần dò giá với quản lý giá tủ.`,
                severity: 'error'
              });
            }
      
            // 3. Kiểm tra trùng lặp dữ liệu 100% trong đợt này
            const cabNo = claim.claim_code || '';
            const sku = claim.product_name || claim.sku || '';
            const desc = claim.claim_description || claim.description || '';
            const defect = claim.order_code || claim.defect_code || '';
            const penalty = normalizePenaltyValue(claim.penalty || 'Không có');
            const lpn = claim.lpn || '';
            const cont = claim.cont || claim.container_no || '';
            const period = claim.date || claim.period || '';
            const key = `${cabNo}|${sku}|${desc}|${defect}|${penalty}|${lpn}|${cont}|${period}`.toLowerCase();
            
            const duplicates = keyMap[key] || [];
            if (duplicates.length > 1) {
              const otherDups = duplicates.filter(d => d.id !== claim.id);
              claimExceptions.push({
                type: 'duplicate_claim',
                label: 'Trùng lặp dữ liệu 100%',
                detail: `Phát hiện dòng dữ liệu bị trùng lặp hoàn toàn với dòng khác. Vui lòng đối chiếu ảnh minh chứng bên dưới để xem có bị trùng lặp ảnh hay không.`,
                severity: 'error',
                duplicates: otherDups
              });
            }
      
            // --- TRANSPORT EXCEPTIONS ---
            // 4. Thiếu LPN
            const lpnVal = (claim.lpn || '').trim();
            if (!lpnVal || lpnVal === 'N/A' || lpnVal === 'n/a' || lpnVal === '-') {
              transportIssues.push({
                type: 'missing_lpn',
                label: 'Thiếu LPN',
                detail: 'Claim này chưa có mã LPN (License Plate Number).',
                severity: 'warning'
              });
            }
      
            // 5. Thiếu Container
            const contVal = (claim.cont || claim.container_no || '').trim();
            if (!contVal || contVal === 'N/A' || contVal === 'n/a' || contVal === '-') {
              transportIssues.push({
                type: 'missing_container',
                label: 'Thiếu Container',
                detail: 'Claim này chưa có mã Container (mã vận chuyển).',
                severity: 'warning'
              });
            }
      
            if (claimExceptions.length > 0) {
              claimInfoExceptions.push({ claim, exceptions: claimExceptions });
            }
            if (transportIssues.length > 0) {
              transportExceptions.push({ claim, exceptions: transportIssues });
            }
          });
      
          return {
            claimInfo: claimInfoExceptions,
            transport: transportExceptions,
            totalCount: claimInfoExceptions.length + transportExceptions.length
          };
        }, [filteredClaimsToDisplay, defectCatalog, cabinetPrices]);
        // ============ END EXCEPTION DETECTION LOGIC ============
